The 4 agents run concurrently and each one's completion arrives as a message in this conversation when it finishes — do NOT issue a separate blocking call to wait for them.\n285\t\n286\t**Once all 4 agents have reported completion, read each agent's output file (single message with 4 Read calls):**\n287\t```\n288\tRead tool:\n289\t  file_path: \"{outputFile from that agent's async_launched result}\"\n290\t```\n291\t\n292\t> Allow up to `workflow.subagent_timeout` for the slowest agent to finish before treating it as failed. The timeout is configurable via `workflow.subagent_timeout` in `.planning/config.json` (milliseconds). Default: 300000 (5 minutes). Increase for large codebases or slower models.\n293\t\n294\tEach output file contains that agent's completion confirmation. Parse the confirmation marker (see below) from the file contents.\n295\t\n296\t**Expected confirmation format from each agent:**\n297\t```\n298\t## Mapping Complete\n299\t\n300\t**Focus:** {focus}\n301\t**Documents written:**\n302\t- `.planning/codebase/{DOC1}.md` ({N} lines)\n303\t- `.planning/codebase/{DOC2}.md` ({N} lines)\n304\t\n305\tReady for orchestrator summary.\n306\t```\n307\t\n308\t**What you receive:** Just file paths and line counts. When `${PATH_SCOPE_HINT}` is empty, perform a full-repo scan.\n323\t\n324\tPerform all 4 mapping passes sequentially:\n325\t\n326\t**Pass 1: Tech Focus**\n327\t- Explore package.json/Cargo.toml/go.mod/requirements.txt, config files, dependency trees\n328\t- Write `.planning/codebase/STACK.md` — Languages, runtime, frameworks, dependencies, configuration\n329\t- Write `.planning/codebase/INTEGRATIONS.md` — External APIs, databases, auth providers, webhooks\n330\t\n331\t**Pass 2: Architecture Focus**\n332\t- Explore directory structure, entry points, module boundaries, data flow\n333\t- Write `.planning/codebase/ARCHITECTURE.md` — Pattern, layers, data flow, abstractions, entry points\n334\t- Write `.planning/codebase/STRUCTURE.md` — Directory layout, key locations, naming conventions\n335\t\n336\t**Pass 3: Quality Focus**\n337\t- Explore code style, error handling patterns, test files, CI config\n338\t- Write `.planning/codebase/CONVENTIONS.md` — Code style, naming, patterns, error handling\n339\t- Write `.planning/codebase/TESTING.md` — Framework, structure, mocking, coverage\n340\t\n341\t**Pass 4: Concerns Focus**\n342\t- Explore TODOs, known issues, fragile areas, security patterns\n343\t- Write `.planning/codebase/CONCERNS.md` — Tech debt, bugs, security, performance, fragile areas\n344\t\n345\tUse the same document templates as the `gsd-codebase-mapper` agent. Include actual file paths formatted with backticks.\n346\t\n347\tContinue to verify_output.\n348\t</step>\n349\t\n350\t<step name=\"verify_output\">\n351\tVerify all documents created successfully:\n352\t\n353\t```bash\n354\tls -la .planning/codebase/\n355\twc -l .planning/codebase/*.md\n356\t```\n357\t\n358\t**Verification checklist:**\n359\t- All 7 documents exist\n360\t- No empty documents (each should have >20 lines)\n361\t\n362\tIf any documents missing or empty, note which agents may have failed.\n363\t\n364\tContinue to stamp_codebase_map.\n365\t</step>\n366\t\n367\t<step name=\"stamp_codebase_map\">\n368\tStamp the drift baseline into every document that was just written:\n369\t\n370\t```bash\n371\tgsd_run stamp-codebase-map ${UPDATED_DOCS:+--files \"$UPDATED_DOCS\"}\n372\t```\n373\t\n374\tThis writes `last_mapped_commit: <HEAD sha>` and `last_mapped_at: <date>` into\n375\tthe YAML frontmatter of each `.planning/codebase/*.md` that exists. It runs on\n376\tevery mapping run, incremental (`--paths`) and full alike. `--files` narrows it\n377\tto the documents an Update run actually refreshed; `--paths` needs no narrowing\n378\tbecause all seven are regenerated, just scoped in content.\n379\t\n380\t**Why this is a shell step and not an instruction to the mapper.** The stamp is\n381\tthe only machine-readable freshness marker: the `verify codebase-drift` gate\n382\treads it to decide what to diff HEAD against. The human-readable markers the\n383\tmapper writes (`**Analysis Date:**`, `<!-- refreshed: ... -->`) are restamped\n384\tunconditionally on an Update run, so a mapper that decides its work is already\n385\tdone and rewrites only the dates still looks fresh to a human. Leaving the\n386\tmachine-readable stamp to the same agent reproduces exactly the failure the\n387\tstamp exists to detect. A shell step cannot be skipped by a confident agent.\n388\t\n389\tThe command is non-blocking: it emits `skipped` with a `reason` outside a git\n390\trepo or when no documents exist. Report `stamped` and `commit` in the summary\n391\tif any entry in `failed` is non-empty; otherwise continue silently.\n392\t\n393\tRun in this position, before `commit_codebase_map`, the stamp lands on\n394\tdocuments the mapper just wrote, so its markdown whitespace normalization is\n395\tfolded into the same commit. It serves recruiters/hiring managers and the design-engineering community equally: personality on the surface, key information (experience, projects, contact) reachable in seconds.\n8\t\n9\t**Core Value:** The site must feel crafted and memorable (tactile, physics-based motion and strong visual identity) while key info stays instantly accessible, on mobile as much as desktop.\n10\t\n11\t### Constraints\n12\t\n13\t- **Tech stack**: Vite + React + TypeScript + Tailwind — required by motion-primitives and react-spring\n14\t- **Hosting**: GitHub Pages — static output only, needs Actions workflow or a built-output branch\n15\t- **Platform**: Mobile must be excellent — touch interactions, performance on phones\n16\t- **Accessibility/perf**: Assumed baseline: `prefers-reduced-motion` respected, good Lighthouse scores (to confirm with user)\n17\t- **Process**: Detailed documentation before coding; research and surface findings for tooling decisions\n18\t\n19\t<!-- GSD:project-end -->\n20\t\n21\t<!-- GSD:stack-start source:research/STACK.md -->\n22\t\n23\t## Technology Stack\n24\t\n25\t## Bottom Line\n26\t\n27\t## Recommended Stack\n28\t\n29\t### Core Framework\n30\t\n31\t| Technology | Version (verified 2026-10-08) | Purpose | Why | Confidence |\n32\t|------------|-------------------------------|---------|-----|------------|\n33\t| Vite | `^8.3.4` | Build/dev server | Current `latest`. `create-vite` 9.2.1 scaffolds it. That is acceptable for a portfolio, but it is the marginal price of the \"both libraries\" decision, so use react-spring where it earns it.\n91\t- Full `motion` component is the heavy part (about 53 kB with presence/spring/gesture features). motion.dev's own figures: `motion` about 34 kB, `m` + `LazyMotion` about 4.6 kB initial (+15 kB `domAnimation`, +25 kB `domMax`), `useAnimate` mini about 2.3 kB. **Optional optimization (not tested):** because motion-primitives is copy-pasted source, you could swap `motion.x` for `m.x` and wrap the app in `LazyMotion`. `AnimatePresence`/`layout`/`drag` need `domMax`, which erodes the saving. Do this only if Lighthouse demands it.\n92\t- Code-split below-the-fold sections with `React.lazy` so the hero ships alone. Any 3D must be lazy-loaded (see Gaps).\n93\t- Not measured: runtime frame rate on a real phone. That belongs in an early performance spike.\n94\t\n95\t## The Four Named Tools\n96\t\n97\t### 1. motion-primitives (ibelick) : USE, as a copy-in source library. Confidence: MEDIUM-HIGH\n98\t\n99\t- Only deps are `motion`, `clsx`, `tailwind-merge`, `lucide-react`, plus a `@/lib/utils` `cn()` helper. **No shadcn init required.**\n100\t- Components are served as shadcn-registry JSON at `https://motion-primitives.com/c/<name>.json` (HTTP 200 verified).\n101\t- The official CLI is `npx motion-primitives@latest add <name>`. It works (tested) but the npm package is stale (0.1.0, March 2025) and **drops files into `./components/motion-primitives/` at the project root, not `src/`**. Either move them to `src/components/motion-primitives/` or skip the CLI and copy the registry JSON content. Decide once and document it.\n102\t- Needs the `@` alias in both `vite.config.ts` (`resolve.alias`) and `tsconfig.app.json` (`paths: { \"@/*\": [\"./src/*\"] }`). With TS 6/7, `paths` works **without** `baseUrl` (verified in the probe). shadcn's Vite doc still says to add `baseUrl`. You do not need it.\n103\t\n104\t### 2. react-spring : USE, scoped. Confidence: HIGH\n105\t\n106\t- Install `@react-spring/web@~10.1.2`. Import `useSpring, useSprings, useTrail, useChain, useTransition, animated, config` from `@react-spring/web`.\n107\t- Version note: `react-spring` (the umbrella, 10.0.4) is behind and v11.0.0-beta (beta.1 released 2026-10-01) is **removing it**, going ESM-only, and deprecating `useTransition` in favor of new `usePresence`/`usePresenceList`. Stay on 10.1.x stable. Avoid `useTransition` for new core UI (use motion's `AnimatePresence`) so the v11 migration stays trivial.\n108\t- Reduced motion is built in: `useReducedMotion` is exported from `@react-spring/web` (confirmed in the type definitions), and the global flag `Globals.assign({ skipAnimation: true })` snaps every spring to its target. Wire it once (snippet below).\n109\t- Dependencies are tiny and tree-shakable. Measured at about +17 kB gz for `useSpring/useTrail/useSprings`.\n110\t\n111\t### 3. realtimecolors.com : USE as a design tool, with caveats. Confidence: HIGH\n112\t\n113\t- The seed (`050315 / fbfbfe / 2f27ce / dedcff / 433bff`, Inter/Inter) is Text, Background, Primary, Secondary, Accent. Note `050315` on `fbfbfe` is the **light** theme pairing (dark text on light bg). The tool's light/dark toggle (Alt+T) lets you design both modes in one session. The project requires light and dark, so design both explicitly rather than inverting.\n114\t- It shows AA/AAA contrast traffic lights per role. Use them as the accessibility gate for text and interactive colors.\n115\t- Exports: CSS variables, Tailwind CSS, SCSS, and custom templates, in HEX/RGB/HSL/OKLAB/OKLCH. The tool lists a `tailwind.config.js` style Tailwind export and does not mention v4. **Export \"CSS\" with OKLCH and paste into `@theme`**, not the Tailwind-config export:\n116\t- Its type-scale presets (1.067 to 1.618) map to a `--text-*` ladder in `@theme`.\n117\t- The user wants alternatives explored: do that in the design phase (Realtime Colors' scheme generators: analogous/complementary/triadic, plus randomize with Space). Keep exactly 5 role tokens plus derived tints so swapping palettes later is a single-block edit.\n118\t\n119\t### 4. godly.design/posts : USE as inspiration only, not a dependency. Confidence: MEDIUM\n120\t\n121\t- `godly.design/posts` is a curated **feed of linked social posts** (mostly X links to designers' UI interaction clips), with category nav (Websites, Tools, Templates, etc.). No dates, tags, RSS, or API were visible. Treat it as a mood-board source: capture screenshots/links into the planning docs, then extract motion patterns (spring feel, press states, cursor effects) into a written reference list before coding. (Probe also noted `godly.website` currently 301-redirects to `recent.design`, so the brand's structure may have shifted. Rely on `godly.design/posts/` and verify when you actually browse.)\n122\t- No code or package implications.\n123\t\n124\t## Division of Responsibility (motion vs react-spring)\n125\t\n126\t| Concern | Owner | Why |\n127\t|---------|-------|-----|\n128\t| Mount/unmount, `AnimatePresence`, exit animations | **motion** | Mature, handles React 19 strict mode and transitions (13.1.1 and 13.4.6 fixes). |\n129\t| Layout and shared-element transitions (`layout`, `layoutId`, Morphing Dialog) | **motion** | react-spring has no equivalent. |\n130\t| Scroll-linked values (`useScroll`, `useTransform`, ScrollProgress, in-view reveals) | **motion** | Already bundled. 13.4.x moved offsets onto hardware-accelerated `ViewTimeline` where supported. |\n131\t| Text/number effects, stagger reveals, all motion-primitives prefabs | **motion** | They are written against it. |\n132\t| Simple drag, `whileTap`, `whileHover` | **motion** | Built in. No extra gesture lib. |\n133\t| Velocity-carrying physics: flick/toss, drag-release with momentum, rubber-band, pointer-chasing follower, squish-on-press toys | **react-spring** | `useSpring` with continuous retargeting is its sweet spot and gives the \"tactile\" signature. |\n134\t| Staggered chains of springs (`useTrail`, `useChain`) for playful sets of elements | **react-spring** | Concise API. |\n135\t| Anything in `@react-spring/three` | **react-spring** | Only if 3D is adopted. |\n136\t\n137\t## Tailwind v4 Notes\n138\t\n139\t- v4 `hover:` only applies under `@media (hover: hover)`, so hover styles never stick after a tap on phones. This matches the \"no desktop-only gestures\" requirement. Do **not** restore v3 behavior with `@custom-variant hover (&:hover)`.\n140\t- Browser floor: Safari 16.4+, Chrome 111+, Firefox 128+ (uses `@property`, `color-mix()`). Fine for this audience.\n141\t- No `tailwind.config.js`; `tw-animate-css` (the shadcn replacement for tailwindcss-animate) is not needed since motion handles animation.\n142\t\n143\t## Dark Mode (covered: Tailwind v4 + tiny script)\n144\t\n145\t## Font Loading\n146\t\n147\t- `import '@fontsource-variable/inter/wght.css'` in `main.tsx`. Verified: it declares `font-display: swap`, `font-weight: 100 900`, and per-script `unicode-range`, so a browser downloads only the **latin subset (48 kB woff2)** for English copy (latin-ext is 85 kB and only fetched if used). Vite emits all subsets to `dist/` (harmless unused files).\n148\t- `@fontsource-variable/inter/opsz.css` adds optical sizing (latin 73 kB). Worth it only if you want display-size tuning.\n149\t- **Preload is not automatic**: a bare `<link rel=\"preload\" href=\"@fontsource-variable/inter/files/...\">` in `index.html` is *not* rewritten by Vite (verified, stays unresolved). If you want a preload, use a small `transformIndexHtml` plugin or `import url from '...woff2?url'`. Skipping preload is acceptable with `font-display: swap`; revisit if Lighthouse flags it.\n150\t- If the design adds a display face from Realtime Colors, use the same Fontsource route (one variable family, latin only), and keep to 2 families total for mobile performance.\n151\t\n152\t## GitHub Pages Deploy\n153\t\n154\t## Alternatives Considered\n155\t\n156\t| Category | Recommended | Alternative | Why Not |\n157\t|----------|-------------|-------------|---------|\n158\t| Framework | Vite + React | Next.js / Astro | Next explicitly out of scope. Astro would be lighter on JS but the user wants React islands everywhere and these two React animation libs. |\n159\t| Animation split | motion for UI choreography + react-spring for signature physics | react-spring only | Loses `layoutId`/`AnimatePresence`/scroll and every motion-primitives component. |\n160\t| Animation split | (same) | motion only | Viable and smaller (saves 17 kB), but the user explicitly wants to try react-spring. Revisit if the spike shows react-spring adds nothing. |\n161\t| react-spring package | `@react-spring/web` | `react-spring` umbrella | Lagging, `deprecated` tag present, removed in v11 beta. |\n162\t| Installing primitives | Copy via registry JSON / CLI, then patch | Wait for an npm package | None exists. Source ownership is the model. |\n163\t| Styling | Tailwind v4 | Tailwind v3.4 | Only for pre-2023 browsers. motion-primitives and shadcn are on v4. |\n164\t| TypeScript | 6.0.x | 7.0.x | 7.0.2 compiled the probe cleanly, so it is available, but v6 is the scaffold default. Take 7 post-launch. |\n165\t| Dark mode | Tailwind data-attr + inline script | next-themes | Next-oriented abstraction we do not need. |\n166\t| Fonts | Fontsource self-hosted | Google Fonts `<link>` | Third-party request, render-blocking, privacy. |\n167\t\n168\t## What NOT to Use\n169\t\n170\t| Do not use | Why |\n171\t|------------|-----|\n172\t| `react-spring` umbrella package | Deprecated tag, removed in v11. Use `@react-spring/web`. |\n173\t| `react-spring` v11 beta | Beta, breaking `onRest`/`immediate` semantics. Wait for stable. |\n174\t| Raw `framer-motion` imports | motion 14 depends on `framer-motion` internally. Always import from `motion/react` so there is one copy. |\n175\t| motion-primitives `Cursor`, `Magnetic`, `Tilt`, `Spotlight` as *primary* interactions | Mouse-only. They are desktop garnish. |\n176\t| `shadcn init` | Unneeded; adds Radix/CVA you will not use. |\n177\t| `tailwind.config.js`, `@tailwind base/components/utilities` | v3 patterns. |\n178\t| `@use-gesture/react` by default | Last published March 2024, and motion already provides drag/tap/hover. Only add if a react-spring-driven drag cannot be done well otherwise. |\n179\t| `vite-plugin-image-optimizer` as the primary image tool | Compress-only (no srcset/format variants). Last publish Oct 2025. |\n180\t| Hard-coded absolute asset paths | Break when `base` changes. |\n181\t| Both libraries animating the same element/property | The one real coexistence hazard. |\n182\t\n183\t## Gaps: capabilities the four tools do NOT cover\n184\t\n185\t| Gap | Already covered? | Candidate A (default) | Candidate B | Notes |\n186\t|-----|------------------|-----------------------|-------------|-------|\n187\t| **Scroll-driven animation** | **Yes, by `motion`** (`useScroll`, `useTransform`, `scroll()`, motion-primitives ScrollProgress/InView) | motion `useScroll` (+0 kB) | GSAP ScrollTrigger (+44 kB gz measured). GSAP is now fully free incl. plugins per gsap.com. | Only choose GSAP for pinned/scrubbed multi-step timelines. Not needed for six sections. |\n188\t| **Smooth scroll** | No | **None (native scroll)** | Lenis 1.3.26 (+5.6 kB measured). `lenis/react` provides `ReactLenis`. | Lenis smoothing is **off on touch by default** (`syncTouch: false`), honors reduced motion by default (`respectReducedMotion: true`), but needs `anchors: true` for in-page nav and community reports it can feel unlike native scroll and conflict with focus/keyboard behavior. Smooth scroll is a taste item, not a requirement. Default: skip until the design wants it. |\n189\t| **3D / WebGL** | No | **None** (use CSS 3D via Tilt and react-spring transforms for tactility) | (a) OGL 1.0.11 for one hero shader/blob (+12.8 kB measured); (b) three 0.186 + @react-three/fiber 9.8.1 (+241 kB gz measured, peer `react >=19 <19.4`) with `@react-spring/three` | R3F is the one thing in this list that can blow the mobile budget. section nav pill (fixed bottom) | `src/layout/PillNav.tsx` |\n| Footer | Site footer | `src/layout/Footer.tsx` |\n| Nav items | Section id and label list used by nav and scroll-spy | `src/layout/nav-items.ts` |\n| Hero | Landing block: name, tagline, resume and copy-email CTAs | `src/sections/Hero/Hero.tsx` |\n| Experience / Projects / About / Skills / Education / Contact | One section each | `src/sections/<Name>/<Name>.tsx` |\n| Project detail | Single native `<dialog>` for a project | `src/sections/Projects/ProjectDialog.tsx` |\n| Project card | Sleeve card in the projects grid | `src/sections/Projects/Sleeve.tsx` |\n| Scroll-spy | IntersectionObserver band, click lock, deep-link jump | `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts` |\n| Theme | Read/write `html[data-theme]`, `localStorage`, OS scheme follow | `src/lib/theme.ts`, `src/hooks/useTheme.ts` |\n| Theme toggle | UI control for theme | `src/components/ui/ThemeToggle.tsx` |\n| Toast | Toast provider and presence | `src/components/ui/Toast.tsx`, `src/components/ui/useToast.ts` |\n| Copy email | Clipboard write with fallback, live region | `src/components/ui/useCopyEmail.ts`, `src/lib/copy.ts` |\n| Motion barrel | Single public import surface for animation | `src/anim/index.ts` |\n| Motion preference | Reduced-motion state via context | `src/anim/MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`, `src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts` |\n| Motion features | `LazyMotion` boundary, loads `domMax` in its own chunk | `src/anim/MotionFeatures.tsx`, `src/anim/features.ts` |\n| Declarative animation | Pill morph, nav indicator, text reveal, toast presence | `src/anim/declarative/*.tsx` |\n| Physics animation | Theme thumb (react-spring) | `src/anim/physics/ThemeThumb.tsx` |\n| Motion tokens | Shared timing and easing values | `src/anim/tokens.ts` |\n| Vendored primitives | Copy-in motion-primitives source | `src/components/motion-primitives/text-effect.tsx` |\n| Content schema | Types for all content | `src/content/schema.ts` |\n| Content aggregate | Single `siteContent` object | `src/content/index.ts` |\n\n## Pattern Overview\n\n**Overall:** Single-page React application with a static content layer, a single page composition root, and an enforced animation boundary. No router, no server, no store library.\n\n**Key Characteristics:**\n- One route. `index.html` plus `404.html` are the only HTML entries. `404.html` is a second Rollup input (`vite.config.ts`) that GitHub Pages serves for unknown paths.\n- Content is typed TypeScript data, not a CMS or fetched JSON. Everything is bundled at build time.\n- Animation is isolated behind `src/anim/`. Components do not import motion libraries directly.\n- Theme state lives in the DOM attribute `html[data-theme]`, which is the single source of truth. `localStorage` persists an explicit choice.\n- The motion feature bundle (`domMax`) is split into its own chunk via `src/anim/features.ts` and loaded after first paint.\n- Section lazy-loading: `src/anim/declarative/TextReveal.tsx` lazy-loads `motion-primitives/text-effect` with `React.lazy` and `Suspense`.\n\n## Layers\n\n**Entry and composition:**\n- Purpose: Mount the app and compose sections in order\n- Location: `src/main.tsx`, `src/app/`\n- Contains: `App.tsx`, `Providers.tsx`, `Page.tsx`\n- Depends on: `src/content`, `src/layout`, `src/sections`, `src/anim`, `src/components/ui`, `src/hooks`\n- Used by: Nothing (root)\n\n**Layout:**\n- Purpose: Persistent chrome around sections\n- Location: `src/layout/`\n- Contains: header, pill nav, footer, container, section wrapper, skip link, wordmark, nav item definitions\n- Depends on: `src/anim` (through barrel), `src/hooks`, `src/components/ui`, `src/lib`\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx`\n\n**Sections:**\n- Purpose: Page content blocks\n- Location: `src/sections/<Name>/`\n- Contains: presentational components taking typed props (`profile`, `entries`, `groups`, `links`, `email`)\n- Depends on: `src/anim` barrel, `src/layout/*`, `src/components/ui`, `src/lib`, `src/content/images` for images, and `src/content/schema` types\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx`\n\n**Shared UI and hooks:**\n- Purpose: Reusable controls, icons, toast, theme, scroll-spy\n- Location: `src/components/ui/`, `src/hooks/`, `src/lib/`\n- Contains: `CtaLink.tsx`, `ThemeToggle.tsx`, `Toast.tsx`, icon components in `src/components/ui/icons/`, `useActiveSection.ts`, `useTheme.ts`, `theme.ts`, `copy.ts`, `utils.ts`\n- Depends on: React, browser APIs\n- Used by: layout, sections, app\n\n**Animation:**\n- Purpose: All motion behavior, reduced-motion handling, lazy motion features\n- Location: `src/anim/` and `src/components/motion-primitives/`\n- Contains: barrel `index.ts`, declarative and physics components, preference provider, token file\n- Depends on: `motion/react`, `@react-spring/web`\n- Used by: layout, sections, app providers. Only through the `@/anim` barrel.\n\n**Content:**\n- Purpose: Site copy and structured data\n- Location: `src/content/`\n- Contains: `profile.ts`, `contacts.ts`, `experience.ts`, `projects.ts`, `skills.ts`, `education.ts`, `schema.ts`, `images.ts`, `index.ts`\n- Depends on: `src/assets/images/*` (through `images.ts`)\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx` only (enforced by lint)\n\n**Styles:**\n- Purpose: Design tokens and global CSS\n- Location: `src/styles/`\n- Contains: `index.css` (entry, imports the others), `tokens.css`, `base.css`, `interactions.css`, `not-found.css`\n- Used by: `src/main.tsx` imports `./styles/index.css`\n\n## Data Flow\n\n### Primary Request Path (page load)\n\n1. Browser loads `index.html`; the inline script sets `html[data-theme]` from `localStorage` or the OS scheme before first paint (`index.html`)\n2. Vite-built module `src/main.tsx` imports the font and CSS, then renders `<App />` into `#root` (`src/main.tsx`)\n3. `App` wraps `Page` in `Providers` (`src/app/App.tsx`, `src/app/Providers.tsx`)\n4. `Page` reads `siteContent` from `src/content/index.ts`, extracts the `email` link and the remaining contact links (`src/app/Page.tsx`)\n5. `Page` calls `useActiveSection(SECTION_IDS)` (`src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts`) and passes `activeId` and `lockTo` to `SiteHeader` and `PillNav`\n6. Sections render with typed props. Images resolve to imagetools output via `src/content/images.ts`\n\n### Theme Toggle Path\n\n1. `ThemeToggle` calls `applyTheme` in `src/lib/theme.ts`\n2. The attribute `data-theme` is written first, then `localStorage` (`THEME_STORAGE_KEY`)\n3. `subscribeTheme` observes the attribute with a `MutationObserver`, and reacts to `storage` events and OS `prefers-color-scheme` changes when nothing is stored\n4. `src/hooks/useTheme.ts` exposes the value to React\n\n### Section Navigation Path\n\n1. Desktop `SiteHeader` and mobile `PillNav` call `onNavigate` = `lockTo(id)` (`src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts`)\n2. `lockTo` sets `activeId` immediately and suppresses the observer until `scrollend`, a quiet period, a fallback timer, or a cap timer releases it\n3. The single `IntersectionObserver` band (`rootMargin` built from `--nav-h` and `-60%` bottom) updates `activeId` otherwise\n\n### Copy Email Path\n\n1. Hero and Contact call `useCopyEmail` (`src/components/ui/useCopyEmail.ts`)\n2. `copyText` in `src/lib/copy.ts` tries `navigator.clipboard.writeText`, then falls back to a hidden textarea with `document.execCommand('copy')`\n3. Result is announced through a live region and a toast (`src/components/ui/useToast.ts`, `src/components/ui/Toast.tsx`)\n\n**State Management:**\n- No global store. State is local to hooks and components, plus React context for motion preference and toasts.\n- Theme truth is the DOM attribute, not React state. React reads it through `readTheme()`.\n- Scroll-spy state is a `useState` plus refs (`prevRef`, `intersectingRef`, `lockedRef`, `releaseLockRef`, `recomputeRef`) inside `useActiveSection`.\n\n## Key Abstractions\n\n**`siteContent` aggregate:**\n- Purpose: One typed object holding all site data\n- Examples: `src/content/index.ts`\n- Pattern: Plain `export const` data, typed by `src/content/schema.ts`. `contacts.ts` uses `as const satisfies readonly ContactLink[]`.\n\n**Animation barrel:**\n- Purpose: Stable public API for animation, the only allowed import path\n- Examples: `src/anim/index.ts` (exports `MotionFeatures`, `MotionPreferenceProvider`, `ThemeThumb`, and others)\n- Pattern: Facade. Consumers import `@/anim`, never `motion/react` or `@react-spring/web` directly.\n\n**Vendored primitives:**\n- Purpose: Copy-in source from motion-primitives, owned by this repo\n- Examples: `src/components/motion-primitives/text-effect.tsx`\n- Pattern: Source ownership. Edited in place, not installed from npm. Lint rules are turned off for this directory (`.oxlintrc.json` override).\n\n**Scroll-spy hook:**\n- Purpose: Determine the active section and manage click-lock\n- Examples: `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts` (pure helper `pickActive` is exported for unit tests in `tests/unit/active-section.test.ts`)\n- Pattern: IntersectionObserver with a computed band, plus timers for lock release\n\n**Toast and copy:**\n- Purpose: Transient feedback after copying the email\n- Examples: `src/components/ui/Toast.tsx`, `src/components/ui/useToast.ts`, `src/components/ui/useCopyEmail.ts`, `src/lib/copy.ts`\n- Pattern: Context provider plus a hook. Live region for screen readers.\n\n**Project dialog:**\n- Purpose: Project detail view\n- Examples: `src/sections/Projects/ProjectDialog.tsx`\n- Pattern: Native `<dialog>` with `showModal()` called in an effect (ADR-0006 native path, referenced in the file's comments)\n\n## Entry Points\n\n**Application:**\n- Location: `src/main.tsx`\n- Triggers: Browser loads `index.html` (dev server or built `dist/index.html`)\n- Responsibilities: Font import, global CSS import, root render in `StrictMode`\n\n**Not-found page:**\n- Location: `404.html` (second Rollup input in `vite.config.ts`). Styled by `src/styles/not-found.css`.\n- Triggers: GitHub Pages serves it for unknown paths\n- Responsibilities: Static fallback page\n\n**Build and check entries:**\n- Location: `vite.config.ts`, `scripts/*.mjs`\n- Triggers: `npm run build`, `npm run verify`, CI workflows\n- Responsibilities: bundling, asset checks, budget and policy checks\n\n## Architectural Constraints\n\n- **Threading:** Single-threaded browser main thread. No Web Workers in `src/`.\n- **Global state:** `src/lib/theme.ts` holds a module-level `unsavedChoice` flag. `html[data-theme]` is global DOM state. `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts` holds scroll and timer state per hook instance.\n- **Circular imports:** None observed during exploration.\n- **Import boundaries (enforced by oxlint `no-restricted-imports` in `.oxlintrc.json`):**\n  - `motion/react`, `@react-spring/web`, `framer-motion`, `motion`, `react-spring` are allowed only in `src/anim/` and `src/components/motion-primitives/`\n  - Animation must be imported through `@/anim` barrel only\n  - `src/content` may be imported only by `src/app/Page.tsx`\n  - `src/anim/**` must not import `@/sections`, `@/layout`, or `@/content`\n  - `tests/unit/boundaries.test.ts` checks these rules against fixtures in `tests/fixtures/`\n- **Reduced motion:** Honored through `src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts` and `MotionPreferenceProvider`. Respect this in any new animation.\n- **Static output only:** No runtime server. Any dynamic behavior must run in the browser.\n- **Base path:** Asset URLs must go through `import.meta.env.BASE_URL` or Vite imports, because `VITE_BASE` changes the base.\n\n## Anti-Patterns\n\n### Importing motion libraries directly in a section or layout component\n\n**What happens:** A component imports from `motion/react` or `@react-spring/web` directly.\n**Why it's wrong:** Lint fails (`no-restricted-imports`), and the `tests/unit/boundaries.test.ts` contract breaks. It also bypasses the lazy feature bundle and reduced-motion handling.\n**Do this instead:** Import from `@/anim` (`src/anim/index.ts`). Add a new export to the barrel if a new primitive is needed.\n\n### Importing content from components\n\n**What happens:** A section or layout file imports `@/content` or `src/content/*` directly to read data.\n**Why it's wrong:** Lint restricts content imports to `src/app/Page.tsx`. Data flow goes top-down from `Page`.\n**Do this instead:** Read data in `src/app/Page.tsx` and pass it as typed props, as `Hero`, `Experience`, `Projects`, and others already do.\n\n### Writing theme state into React state only\n\n**What happens:** Setting theme in component state without writing `html[data-theme]`.\n**Why it's wrong:** The inline bootstrap in `index.html` and `subscribeTheme` in `src/lib/theme.ts` both treat the DOM attribute as the source of truth. The two can diverge.\n**Do this instead:** Call `applyTheme` in `src/lib/theme.ts` and read with `readTheme`.\n\n### Importing `@/anim/features` statically\n\n**What happens:** A file imports `src/anim/features.ts` directly, pulling `domMax` into the initial graph.\n**Why it's wrong:** The file exists to keep the motion feature bundle in its own chunk, loaded after first paint (`src/anim/features.ts` header comment).\n**Do this instead:** Use `MotionFeatures` from `src/anim/MotionFeatures.tsx`, which loads the feature bundle through `LazyMotion`.\n\n## Error Handling\n\n**Strategy:** Defensive try/catch around browser APIs that can be blocked or missing. Degrade quietly rather than throw.\n\n**Patterns:**\n- `localStorage` access wrapped in try/catch in `src/lib/theme.ts` and `index.html`. The in-memory choice still applies when storage is blocked.\n- `matchMedia` wrapped in try/catch in `src/lib/theme.ts`. Falls back to `light`.\n- Clipboard write falls back from `navigator.clipboard.writeText` to `document.execCommand('copy')` in `src/lib/copy.ts`.\n- `useActiveSection` does nothing when `IntersectionObserver` is missing. `activeId` stays `null`.\n- Scroll lock has fallback and cap timers so a stuck scroll cannot freeze the indicator.\n- `TextReveal` wraps a lazy import in an error boundary class component (`src/anim/declarative/TextReveal.tsx`).\n\n## Cross-Cutting Concerns\n\n**Logging:** None. No logging library. Console output is not a designed channel.\n**Validation:** Compile-time only, via TypeScript types in `src/content/schema.ts` and `satisfies` checks. No runtime validation library.\n**Authentication:** Not applicable (public static site).\n**Accessibility:** Skip link (`src/layout/SkipLink.tsx`), `aria-labelledby` on sections, live region for copy feedback, `prefers-reduced-motion` honored, tests use `axe-core`.\n**Build-time checks:** `scripts/check-*.mjs` enforce path case, color tokens, contrast pairs, JS budget, absence of phone numbers, and image outputs. These run in `npm run verify`.\n\n---\n\n*Architecture analysis: 2026-11-19*\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2158,"cache_read_input_tokens":73612,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2158,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":16,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"global","speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_01SXPNcCADiohZx1sSJTM551","agent_id":"aa9cfbdac51490013","session_id":"effbf7cc-5267-4ad4-97a9-7643b3fc33c6","uuid":"0f54c869-28d4-4290-9639-11b28dd48f49","timestamp":"2026-11-20T03:06:02.100Z","request_id":"req_011ChDSUY4ZWNGyqjSqPY8jS","subagent_type":"gsd-codebase-mapper","task_description":"Map codebase tech and arch"}
